# Agents · D · part 13

966 entries, Demosthenes to deploy-agent-v2_0. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **Demosthenes**: Prompt engineer. Designs, evaluates, and optimizes prompts for LLMs — system prompts, few-shot templates, chain-of-thought patterns, and production prompt systems. Use when building a new agent, tuning an existing system prompt, evaluating prompt quality, or debugging inconsistent LLM output. Reports honesty ledger on completion. ([wrg32786/aigent-os](https://github.com/wrg32786/aigent-os/blob/HEAD/vault/agents/demosthenes.md) · ★26)
- **demotie-analist**: Alleen aanroepen als de leerharnas daarom vraagt (automatisch omlaag voor een hoofdvaardigheid). Analyseert de mislukte taken van een vaardigheid, koppelt ze aan deelpatronen (microvaardigheden) en beslist of een microvaardigheid of de hoofdvaardigheid een niveau zakt. Alleen lezen. ([piethanegraaf/piethanegraaf.github.io](https://github.com/piethanegraaf/piethanegraaf.github.io/blob/HEAD/harness/agents/demotie-analist.nl.md))
- **demotie-analist**: Only call when the learning harness asks for it (automatic demotion of a parent skill). Analyses the failed tasks of a skill, links them to sub-patterns (microskills) and decides whether a microskill or the parent skill goes down a level. Read only. ([piethanegraaf/piethanegraaf.github.io](https://github.com/piethanegraaf/piethanegraaf.github.io/blob/HEAD/harness/agents/demotie-analist.en.md))
- **demystifier**: Explain concepts, designs and systems through a live presentation with anchored discussion. Verifies factual claims, uses diagrams and math when useful, and revises explanations in response to the human. Use when asked to explain, teach, demystify, walk through or deep-dive a topic interactively. Run as the main agent with copilot --agent agents:demystifier; uses the standalone presentation skill. ([ztnel/agents](https://github.com/ztnel/agents/blob/HEAD/com.github.copilot/agents/demystifier.agent.md))

- **dendritic**: Finds behaviour that is MISSING rather than broken - states a screen forgets, platform conventions the product ignores, one front door behaving unlike another. Use for new features, unexplored screens, or "what are we not testing". Stem cell: grows into this project's own version on first run. ([proxysoul/SoulStack](https://github.com/proxysoul/SoulStack/blob/HEAD/agents/dendritic.md) · ★30)
- **dendritic-architecture-reviewer**: Reviews the dendritic architecture infrastructure itself: module-containers, capability flags, import-tree usage, lib/ helpers, and host definitions. Identifies missing flags, unused flags, and structural improvements. ([fred-drake/nix](https://github.com/fred-drake/nix/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dendritic-architecture-reviewer.md) · ★28)
- **dendritic-core**: - **Single tree of modules**: Every feature file exports under `flake.*` (e.g. `flake.modules.nixos.&lt;name&gt;` or `flake.modules.homeManager.&lt;name&gt;`); nothing imports siblings directly. - **Host declarations**: Each `modules/hosts/&lt;id&gt;/default.nix` contributes a `hosts.&lt;id&gt;` record with `metadata` and `modules`. Hosts list named aspects in `modules`; the collector resolves the NixOS or Darwin aspect ([fbosch/nixos](https://github.com/fbosch/nixos/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/dendritic-core.md) · ★2)
- **dendritic-darwin-reviewer**: Reviews Darwin/macOS modules for dendritic pattern compliance. Checks that modules/darwin/ implementations are registered via modules/features/darwin-*.nix, per-host configs are thin, and capability flags are used properly. ([fred-drake/nix](https://github.com/fred-drake/nix/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dendritic-darwin-reviewer.md) · ★28)
- **dendritic-feature-reviewer**: Reviews modules/features/*.nix for dendritic pattern compliance. Checks that every feature module self-registers into my.modules.{nixos,darwin,home-manager}, uses capability flags (config.my.*) with lib.mkIf guards, and follows the correct flake-parts module signature (_: { ... }). ([fred-drake/nix](https://github.com/fred-drake/nix/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dendritic-feature-reviewer.md) · ★28)
- **dendritic-hm-reviewer**: Reviews Home Manager modules for dendritic pattern compliance. Checks that implementations in modules/home-manager/ are properly registered via wrapper modules in modules/features/, don't contain scattered host-specific logic, and use capability flags instead of platform checks where possible. ([fred-drake/nix](https://github.com/fred-drake/nix/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dendritic-hm-reviewer.md) · ★28)
- **dendritic-services-reviewer**: Reviews server services (modules/services/) and Colmena deployment configs (colmena/) for dendritic pattern compliance. Checks whether services should be registered as deferred NixOS modules, whether Colmena host configs are thin, and whether there's config duplication across hosts. ([fred-drake/nix](https://github.com/fred-drake/nix/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dendritic-services-reviewer.md) · ★28)
